# ── LARA Helpers (auto-injected) ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']

# ── LARA Helpers (auto-injected) ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']

# ── LARA Helpers (auto-injected) ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']

# Initialize variables to track the most-played song
max_play_count = -1
most_played_song_title = None

# Loop through each album and its songs to find the song with the highest play_count
for album in album_library:
    for song_id in album['song_ids']:
        song = call_api('spotify', 'show_song', token, song_id=song_id)
        print(f"Checking song: {song['title']} with play_count: {song['play_count']}")
        if song['play_count'] > max_play_count:
            max_play_count = song['play_count']
            most_played_song_title = song['title']

APP: spotify, phone
REASONING:
  - Scope: I will iterate all playlists in the Spotify library to find liked songs, then for each liked song, check existing reviews and update or create a 5-star review.
  - Metric: The key property is 'like_count' for playlists and 'like' status for songs, confirmed by show_playlist_library API.
  - Ambiguities: The task specifies "liked" songs, which I interpret as songs that are marked as liked in Spotify. The rating update is based on existing reviews or creating new ones.
  - API quirks: The show_song_reviews API returns a list of reviews, and updating a review requires the review ID. Creating a review on a song with an existing review results in an error, so I will check first.
PLAN:
  1. Use login_to_app('spotify') to get the access token.
  2. Use call_api('spotify', 'show_playlist_library', token) to fetch all playlists.
  3. For each playlist, iterate through all song IDs in 'song_ids'.
  4. For each song ID:
     - Use call_api('spotify', 'show_song', token, song_id) to get song details.
     - Use call_api('spotify', 'show_liked_songs', token) to verify if the song is liked.
     - If liked, proceed to review:
       a. Use call_api('spotify', 'show_song_reviews', token, song_id) to get existing reviews.
       b. If reviews exist, get review ID and use call_api('spotify', 'update_song_review', token, review_id, rating=5) to update.
       c. If no reviews, use call_api('spotify', 'review_song', token, song_id, rating=5) to create a new review.
  5. Every step involving API calls will be executed accordingly.
  6. Use call_api('spotify', 'logout', token) after completing all reviews.

# ── LARA Helpers (auto-injected) ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']

# ── LARA Helpers (auto-injected) ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']


def call_api(app_name, api_name, token, **kwargs):
    """Call any AppWorld API with access_token injected.
    Works for both read and write/update operations.

# For each liked song, check existing reviews and update or create review
for playlist in playlists:
    for song_id in playlist.get('song_ids', []):
        if any(ls['song_id'] == song_id for ls in liked_songs):
            # Fetch existing reviews for the song
            reviews = call_api('spotify', 'show_song_reviews', token, song_id=song_id)
            print(f"Reviews for song ID {song_id}:", reviews)
            if reviews:
                # Update the existing review to 5 stars
                review_id = reviews[0].get('review_id')
                call_api('spotify', 'update_song_review', token, review_id=review_id, rating=5)
                print(f"Updated review {review_id} to 5 stars.")
            else:
                # Create a new review with 5 stars
                call_api('spotify', 'review_song', token, song_id=song_id, rating=5)
                print(f"Created new 5-star review for song ID {song_id}.")

APP: spotify
REASONING:
  - Scope: I will iterate over all songs in the user's Spotify library to identify which ones are not liked.
  - Metric: The key property is 'liked' (from show_song_library API response) to filter songs that are not liked.
  - Ambiguities: The task asks to "decrease" the rating to 1 if already higher, but the API only supports setting a rating directly. I will check existing reviews and update or create as needed.
  - API quirks: The review APIs are separate for getting, updating, creating, and deleting reviews. Deleting a review before creating a new one ensures the rating is set to 1.
  
PLAN:
  1. Use login_to_app('spotify') to get the access token.
  2. Call show_song_library with the token to fetch all songs in the library.
  3. For each song, call show_song_review to check if a review exists:
     - If a review exists:
       - If the rating > 1, call delete_song_review to remove it.
       - Then call review_song with rating=1 to set the lowest rating.
     - If no review exists:
       - Call review_song with rating=1 to create a new review with the lowest rating.
  4. Every step that reads or writes reviews will be explicitly named with the correct field names.
  5. Finish by confirming all songs are rated 1 star.

Every step that reads or sorts by a field will explicitly mention the field name.

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']

    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']

# Fetch reviews for each song to find existing review_id and update rating if needed

    reviews = call_api('spotify', 'show_song_reviews', token, song_id=song['song_id'])

        current_rating = review.get('rating', 0)
        if current_rating < 4:
            # Corrected API call to update review using 'song_review_id'

# ── LARA Helpers (auto-injected) ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']

# ── LARA Helpers (auto-injected) ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']

from datetime import datetime, timedelta

# Define date range for today and yesterday
today = datetime.now()
yesterday = today - timedelta(days=1)
date_format = "%Y-%m-%d"

# Filter transactions from today or yesterday involving Glenn Burton
relevant_transactions = []
for t in transactions:
    t_date = datetime.strptime(t['created_at'], "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S")
    if (t_date.date() == today.date() or t_date.date() == yesterday.date()):
        sender_name = t['sender'].get('name', '')
        receiver_name = t['receiver'].get('name', '')
        if 'Glenn Burton' in [sender_name, receiver_name]:
            relevant_transactions.append(t)

print('Relevant transactions:', relevant_transactions)

# ── LARA Helpers (auto-injected) ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']

# ── LARA Helpers (auto-injected) ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']

# Fetch details for all song IDs
songs_details = [call_api('spotify', 'show_song', token, song_id=sid) for sid in all_song_ids]
# Prepare list of dicts with 'Title' and 'Artists'
songs_list = []
for song in songs_details:
    title = song.get('title', '')
    artists = song.get('artists', [])
    artist_names = '|'.join(artist.get('name', '') for artist in artists)
    songs_list.append({'Title': title, 'Artists': artist_names})
print("Sample of processed songs:", songs_list[:5])

print("CSV backup created at '~/backups/spotify.csv'")

# ── LARA Helpers (auto-injected) ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']

[CODE — ReAct step 6 / attempt 1]
# Initialize list to hold song details
songs_details = []

# Fetch details for each song ID
for song_id in song_ids:
    song_info = call_api('spotify', 'show_song', token, song_id=song_id)
    title = song_info.get('title', 'Unknown Title')
    artists_list = song_info.get('artists', [])
    artists = '|'.join([artist.get('name', 'Unknown Artist') for artist in artists_list])
    songs_details.append({'title': title, 'artists': artists})

# Prepare CSV content with headers
csv_lines = ['Title,Artists']
for song in songs_details:
    csv_lines.append(f"{song['title']},{song['artists']}")

csv_content = '\n'.join(csv_lines)
print("CSV content prepared.")

# ── LARA Helpers (auto-injected) ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']


def call_api(app_name, api_name, token, **kwargs):

    Works for both read and write/update operations.

    return result['access_token']

import csv

# Prepare CSV data
csv_output = io.StringIO()

writer.writerow(["Title", "Artists"])

for song in song_details:
    title = song.get('title', '')
    artists = song.get('artists', [])
    artist_names = "|".join([artist.get('name', '') for artist in artists])
    writer.writerow([title, artist_names])

csv_content = csv_output.getvalue()
print(csv_content)

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']

# ── LARA Helpers (auto-injected) ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']

    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']

# ── LARA Helpers (auto-injected) ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']

# Filter transactions from the last 5 days
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

# Current date for filtering
current_date = datetime.strptime('2023-03-22T12:04:01', '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S')
five_days_ago = current_date - timedelta(days=5)

# Filter transactions within last 5 days
recent_transactions = [
    t for t in transactions
    if datetime.strptime(t['created_at'], '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S') >= five_days_ago
]
print('Recent transactions:', recent_transactions)

def login_to_app(app_name):

    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""

# ── LARA Helpers (auto-injected) ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']

# For each roommate transaction, check comments, add comment if needed, and like
for tx in roommate_transactions:
    tx_id = tx['transaction_id']
    # Fetch existing comments for the transaction
    comments = call_api('venmo', 'show_transaction_comments', token, transaction_id=tx_id)
    print(f"Comments for transaction {tx_id}:", comments)
    # Check if "Thank you so!" comment exists
    if not any(comment.get('text') == "Thank you so!" for comment in comments):
        # Add comment
        call_api('venmo', 'create_transaction_comment', token, transaction_id=tx_id, comment="Thank you so!")
        print(f"Added comment to transaction {tx_id}")
    # Like the transaction
    call_api('venmo', 'like_transaction', token, transaction_id=tx_id)
    print(f"Liked transaction {tx_id}")

# ── LARA Helpers (auto-injected) ─────────────────────────────────────────────

def login_to_app(app_name):
    """Login to any AppWorld app. Returns access_token string.
    Handles email lookup + credential lookup + login automatically."""
    email = apis.supervisor.show_profile()['email']
    accounts = apis.supervisor.show_account_passwords()
    cred = next((a for a in accounts if a['account_name'] == app_name), None)
    if not cred:
        raise ValueError(f"No credentials found for app '{app_name}'")
    result = getattr(apis, app_name).login(username=email, password=cred['password'])
    return result['access_token']

# Collect all unique artist IDs from the playlist tracks
artist_ids = set()
for playlist in playlists_details:
    for song in playlist['songs']:

            artist_ids.add(artist_id)

for artist_id in artist_ids:
    artist_info = call_api('spotify', 'show_artist', token, artist_id=artist_id)
    follower_count = artist_info.get('follower_count', 0)
    artist_followers[artist_id] = follower_count
    print(f"Artist ID {artist_id}: follower count {follower_count}")
